<result>Prefix is 2272 tokens, under the 2300 budget but above the 2100 aim. Stopping at the first passing result made it 2272, not 2100, and I didn't drop any item from the must-stay-true list. Nothing is committed.

**Measuring.** `data/d5/measure_prefix.py` works tokenizer-only, with no model load and no GPU. It reproduces your numbers: HEAD request prints prefix=1888 and `/tmp/requests.long.json` prints prefix=3552.

**Measured:** `prefix=2272 rows=80 max_total=2321 min_total=2311`. `render_check.py` prints `prefix_lengths=[2272]`, the same number. Request sha256 is `8136e642782358d483c9ff875fa598770363c07f2e708c38873e33d91d4763d0`.

**Proof it's description-only.** With all `description` keys stripped, the old and new `mcp-design.json` are equal as JSON and equal as `json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False)` without sort_keys. `check_design.py` prints PASS.

**Tool descriptions:**
- 空调调节: 控制空调。仅温度动作可带位置。
- 车窗调节: 控制车窗。开大、降下来=开口变大;关小、往上收=开口变小。
- 天窗调节: 控制天窗,无位置。
- 座椅: 调节座椅加热/通风档位。
- 媒体播放: 播放指定内容或控制当前播放。
- 导航: 导航。
- 音量调节: 调节音量,1-40档。
- 灯光: 控制氛围灯/阅读灯。

**Position text.** Window and seat use `省略=主驾,勿填driver;co_driver副驾;rear_left左后;rear_right右后;all_front前排;all_rear后排;left左侧;right右侧;all全车`. 空调 prefixes it with `仅温度动作可用;`.

**Parameter descriptions:**
- 空调:
  - action: `set_temperature=设为value;increase/decrease=升/降0.5度,无value;step_up/step_down=没说数量时加/减一档(fan_step_up/fan_step_down同);fan_set=设为level;fan_increase/fan_decrease=增/减value档。`
  - level: `1-7,仅fan_set。`
  - value: `set_temperature=16-32,步长0.5;fan_increase/fan_decrease=档数。`
  - position: the shared text above. The old awkward wording is gone.
- 车窗:
  - action: `open/maximum=全开;close/minimum=全关;set=设为percent;increase/decrease=开大/关小percent;step_up/step_down=没说数量时开大/关小`
  - percent: `0=全关,100=全开。`
  - position: the shared text.
- 天窗: same action text as 车窗. percent: `0=全关,100=全开;默认10。`
- 座椅:
  - action: `set=设为level;increase/decrease=增/减level档;step_up/step_down=没说数量时加/减一档;minimum=1档。`
  - position: the shared text.
- 媒体播放 action: `search_play=说了具体歌名/歌手/风格,按query搜索播放;play=没说具体内容。`
- 导航 route_strategy: `路线偏好编号,没特别要求不填。`
- 音量调节:
  - action: `set=设为value;increase/decrease=增/减value档;step_up/step_down=没说数量时加/减一档。`
  - value: `档位或变化档数。`
  - module: `省略=media媒体。`
- 灯光:
  - action: `set_brightness=设为brightness;increase/decrease=调亮/调暗一点,无value;set_color仅氛围灯;set_color_temp仅阅读灯。`
  - value: `仅set_color_temp的色温,如 暖、4000K。`

**Dropped or shortened, none of it on the must-stay-true list:**
- The separate `increase/decrease` vs `step_up/step_down` wording is now one shared pattern. The "没说数量" cue is kept, but the "一点/一些" examples are gone from most tools.
- The 空调 list of actions that take no position (fan, direction, mode, circulation, defrost, on/off) is cut. Only "仅温度动作可带位置" remains.
- 空调 `maximum`/`minimum` and 座椅 `maximum=3档` are not described. The 座椅 `level` description is cut, so only the schema's min/max remain.
- `left`/`right` lost their "(左前+左后)" gloss.
- 导航 has no action, destination or category descriptions, and the tool description is just "导航。".
- Parameter descriptions are removed for 空调 `mode`, 座椅 `feature`, 媒体 `query`, 灯光 `feature`/`brightness`/`color`, and 音量 `value`'s set-vs-change split.
- 车窗/天窗 `percent` no longer says "set 是目标/increase 是变化量", because the action text carries it.
- The old advice to prefer `open`/`close` over `maximum`/`minimum` is gone.

**Stale DESIGN.md text.** The sentence in DESIGN.md that says the descriptions "建议优先用 open/close" and explain each value is no longer accurate. You told me to change nothing else, so I left it. I added only the budget sentence, with prefix=2272 and max_total=2321.

**Files in `docs/proposals/mcp-tool-redesign-r217`:** `mcp-design.json`, `DESIGN.md`, `data/d5/requests-8tools-v1.json` (rebuilt) and the new `data/d5/measure_prefix.py`. I removed my scratch files from /tmp and left `/tmp/design.long.json`, `/tmp/requests.long.json` and `/tmp/requests.head.json` for you to delete.</result>

<result>The check reproduces: 0.9454, same packed and baseline losses (4.16878 / 4.19059). I read it as mostly (a) with some (b), and the data do not support (c). Nothing was trained or committed, no threshold or `train_packed.py` change, and the temp files are deleted. `train_packed.py` already showed ` M` in `git status` before I started.

Full-gradient cosine, all 504 tensors, flattened, float64, packed vs unpacked (seed-217 order):

| group | rows (order positions) | base loss | full cos | full rel diff | cos on check tensor |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| row 0 alone | 1903 | 10.131 | 1.0000 | 0.000 | 1.0000 |
| row 1 alone | 1218 | ~0 | 0.9998 | 0.024 | 0.9997 |
| row 2 alone | 1238 | ~0 | 0.9688 | 0.249 | 0.9702 |
| row 3 alone | 523 | 6.544 | 1.0000 | 0.000 | 1.0000 |
| pos 0-4 together | | 4.169 | 0.9855 | 0.170 | 0.9454 |
| pos 4-8 | | 4.209 | 0.99983 | 0.021 | 0.9998 |
| pos 8-12 | | 1.442 | 0.99916 | 0.062 | 0.9999 |
| pos 12-16 | | 3.818 | 0.99955 | 0.030 | 0.9994 |

- **Check tensor:** `base_model.model.model.language_model.layers.0.self_attn.q_proj.lora_B.default.weight`. It is the first of 252 nonzero-gradient tensors, but ranks only 216 of 504 by norm.
- **Its numbers (pos 0-4):** baseline gradient norm 0.287, packed 0.259. The norm of (packed − baseline) is 0.329 of the baseline norm. The other groups give 0.035-0.053.
- **Top-5 tensors by baseline gradient norm (pos 0-4):** relative difference 0.155, 0.028, 0.018, 0.013, 0.056. Cosines are 0.988, 0.9996, 0.9999, 0.9999, 0.9984. Only `layers.6.mlp.down_proj.lora_B` is visibly off, at norm 2.35.
- **Per-tensor cosine over the 252 nonzero tensors (pos 0-4):** minimum 0.879, 10th percentile 0.933, median 0.995. In the other groups the minimum is 0.9986 or higher.
- **Packing order:** a packed rerun in the same order is identical (cos 1.0000). Packing the same four rows in reversed order moves the full gradient by 5.3% (cos 0.9986) and the check tensor by 9% (cos 0.9962). Packed-reversed vs baseline gives check cos 0.968. For pos 4-8 the same test gives about 3%.

Reading:
- **Not (c):** the three other groups of 4 are at 0.9992-0.9998 full cosine, and full-gradient agreement at pos 0-4 is 0.9855. There is no systematic mismatch.
- **(a) fits:** this group contains two rows with baseline loss near 0 (a gradient norm around 1e-7 on the check tensor) and two high-loss rows (10.1 and 6.5). Only the high-loss rows and the shared prefix carry gradient. Single-row packing is exact on the two high-loss rows, so the packing logic itself is not the cause.
- **(b) is plausible:** how the four-row gradient sums over the shared prefix depends on packing order (5-9% moves). That points to bf16 accumulation noise, which is large relative to a small tensor like this one.
- **Not settled:** I have no fp32 or CPU reference. The baseline is also bf16, so I can't tell which of baseline or packed is closer to the true gradient. I only checked 4 groups plus 4 singles, so I can't say how often a bad group occurs. A single-row packed test cannot show a prefix-summation problem, because it uses the same layout as the baseline.
- **Note:** the trainer's cosine is on one float32 tensor. A float32 flattened cosine over the whole vector gave 1.007 on row 0, so I switched to float64.</result>

<summary>Agent "Map existing data-generation pipeline for reuse" finished</summary>
<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>
<result>1. Pipeline, as recorded by the spec/builder/filter/build files; I did not re-run any of it.
- Early rounds: `D/data/d2/{ac,window,other,refuse}/gen_*.py` write `*-new.jsonl`. `D/data/d4/build_train_v2.py:40-141` merges them with `D/data/v2/train-new.jsonl`, which `D/data/map_labels.py:50-115` produced by remapping the old r214 train set. Output is `train-v2.jsonl` (2424 rows).
- The `d7/r2..r5` rounds feed `build_train_v3.py`, `v4.py` and `v5.py`. Each asserts the previous file's sha and copies it verbatim (`build_train_v6.py:23,77-79`), then appends a supplement.
- Rounds r6/r7/r8 (e.g. r8): `SPEC-r8.md` + `SPEC-r8a/b*.md`, then `r8/a_zonewin/build_r8a.py` (or `build_r8b.py`) + `vocab_r8a.json`, then `selftest.sh`. The builder writes `r8a-new.jsonl` and `template_ids.tsv`.
- `d7/r8/filter_r8.py` runs `audit_labels.py`, `verify_d2.py --reuse-old-ok` and `check_d6_leak07.py`, applies its own rules, and writes `r8a-filtered.jsonl` and `FILTER-r8.json`.
- A sol review produces `DROPS-r8.json` (whole-row drops by index into the filtered files). `d4/build_train_v6.py` reads `*-filtered.jsonl` and `DROPS-r8.json` and writes `train-v6.jsonl` and `train-v6.manifest.json`.
- Row fields luna's builders write: `utterance, new_tool, new_args, new_assistant_text, rule_id, class, source, template_id` (`build_r8a.py:18-22`). `build_train_v6.py:48` adds `class_orig` and `unit`.
- The `new_assistant_text` comes from `ml.emit` at `build_r8a.py:21`. `map_labels.py:33-34` defines `emit`.

2. Your belief is wrong in one respect. Luna writes no per-utterance structured intent file.
- Luna writes a Python builder plus a vocab JSON of templates with placeholders (`vocab_r8a.json`, e.g. `"{z}有点冷，帮我升高一点"`, `{z}`, `{v}`, `{p}`).
- The (tool, args) comes from the builder's loop variables: `build_r8a.py:29-62`, `build_r8b.py:10-13`. `emit` renders the text from those. The same variables fill the template, so intent and sentence are tied by construction.
- Luna does not write the label text and does not write one intent per sentence.
- Luna also writes the refusal and ask-back replies. They are lists in the vocab file (`vocab_r8b.json`: `ask_replies`, `seat_replies`).

3. Gates:
- `build_train_v6.py:40`: near-duplicate vs E4 (`&gt;= 0.7` difflib ratio). `:41`: vs D6 (`&gt;= 0.7`; `check_d6_leak07.py:13` `THRESH = 0.7`, pool must be 613 sentences, `:23`).
- `build_train_v6.py:44`: exact duplicate vs train-v5. `:47`: `emit` mismatch. `:23`: train-v5 sha. `:77-79`: prefix preserved.
- `build_train_v6.py:62`: prefix/suffix of 4 characters above 12% per (unit, class) with at least 10 rows (`:58`).
- `filter_r8.py:123`: the same 12% rule, plus any 4-char prefix/suffix used by more than 3 rows (`k &gt; 3`).
- `filter_r8.py:138`: (tool,args) call group of more than 3 rows is dropped.
- `filter_r8.py:81-87,104`: D6 `&gt;= 0.7`, duplicate vs train-v5, `position == 'driver'` emitted, duplicate within the file, E4 `&gt;= 0.7`.
- `filter_r8.py:28,99-101`: zone-word to position check.
- `verify_d2.py:167` (hits at `:158`): similarity of 0.8 or more to the old eval sets.
- `verify_d2.py:80`: at least 90% distinct normalised utterances per class.
- `verify_d2.py:92`: near-duplicates of 0.9 or more within a class of 20 or more rows must stay at or below 20%.
- `verify_d2.py:106`: at least 4 distinct refusal replies per class.
- Label validation against `mcp-design.json`: `verify_d2.py:67-69` and `map_labels.py:11-29`.
- Not found in code: the "near-duplicate vs train-v5 at 0.9" gate in `SPEC-r8.md:31`. It is spec text only. The 8-phrasings minimum (`SPEC-r8.md:22`) is also not enforced by code. `selftest.sh:28` only prints a line, and `verify_d2.py:106` enforces 4.
- Also spec-only: the template gate (at most 2 rows per `template_id`, distinct ids at least rows/2), which is enforced inside each builder (`build_r8a.py:86-91`, `build_r8b.py:18-20`).

4. Refusals and ask-backs:
- `new_assistant_text` is the reply string plus `&lt;|im_end|&gt;`, written by luna in the vocab file and attached by the builder (`build_r8b.py:7,10,13`).
- There is no central template store. The reply lists are per vocab file; the early rounds keep them in `gen_*.py` and `d2/refuse/`.

<summary>Agent "Add lr-step-offset for single cosine over epochs" finished</summary>
<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>
<result>The `--lr-step-offset` change is in `train_packed.py` and not committed. It is default-unchanged: with offset 0, all three CPU-only runs match the unedited copy after normalizing timestamps, PIDs and RSS. Both runs of each pair exited 0, and `pgrep` found no `train_packed` or `eval_new` process before the runs. I did not run `--train` or anything on the GPU.

**Diff (26 changed lines):**
- New argument `--lr-step-offset`, int, default 0.
- Line 540 now computes `schedule_lr(step + args.lr_step_offset, ...)`.
- `save_checkpoint` and `save_epoch_checkpoint` take `lr_step_offset=0`. All 6 call sites in the train path pass it. `state["lr_step_offset"]` is written only when non-zero.
- On resume of an incomplete epoch, there is a new assertion in the `incompatible resume` style: `state.get("lr_step_offset", 0) == args.lr_step_offset`.
- `--dry-plan` keeps the keys 0, 4, 5, 75, 150, 151. It now computes them at `i + offset` and drops a key if `i + offset &gt;= total_steps`.

**(b) Default-unchanged proof:**
- I ran `--shuffle-test`, `--static-check` and the `--dry-plan` command with `train-v6.jsonl` on the before and after files.
- The raw outputs differ only in the first torch warning line (`W1109 01:13:04.756 35646` vs `01:13:10.171 35667`, a timestamp and PID). The static-check output also carries `maxrss_mib`, a memory figure, which I masked.
- After masking those, all three outputs are identical (`diff` is empty). The dry-plan lr values are bit-identical.
- The script derives its path from `__file__`, so I ran the unedited copy from a temp file in the same directory and deleted it afterwards.

**(c) Two-epoch plan, `--total-steps 398`:** every dry-plan value equals the independent formula to within 1e-15, and `schedule_lr` itself matches exactly.

| Epoch | Step (global) | lr |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 0 | 2e-05 |
| 1 | 4 | 1e-04 |
| 1 | 5 | 1e-04 |
| 1 | 75 | 9.2374e-05 |
| 1 | 150 | 7.0008e-05 |
| 1 | 198 | 5.1399e-05 |
| 2 | 0 (199) | 5.0999e-05 |
| 2 | 1 (200) | 5.0600e-05 |
| 2 | 100 (299) | 1.4857e-05 |
| 2 | 198 (397) | 1.5975e-09 |

- Epoch-2 first lr is 0.51 of the peak, the cosine midpoint, and it continues from epoch 1 step 198 (5.14e-05) without a restart. The last global step is near zero.
- In the epoch-2 plan the 75, 150 and 151 keys show global steps 274, 349 and 350 (2.2617e-05, 3.7869e-06, 3.6358e-06).

I deleted the /tmp files and the temp copies. The only tracked change is `train_packed.py`. A gitignored `__pycache__/` from my runs remains in `train-r217`.</result>

<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>
<result>The held-out set is built and all five gates pass. `check_heldout.py` exits 0. One deliverable is missing: the tool refused to let me write `REPORT.md` because subagents can't write report files. Its content is below, and you or the parent can save it.

**Rows:** 399 total, 323 call and 76 no-call.
- **By tool:** 空调调节 110 (49 temperature/positioned), 车窗调节 42, 天窗调节 21, 座椅 46 (heating 24, ventilation 22), 灯光 51, 音量调节 23, 媒体播放 16, 导航 14.
- **By family:** colloquial 140, polite 116, count 85, extreme 58.
- **No-call families (rows/distinct replies):** unsupported_feature 13/13, out_of_range_value 13/13, missing_required_ask 13/13, chitchat_no_promise 12/12, unsupported_position 12/12, ambiguous_direction 13/13.
- **Position buckets** (omitted, co_driver, rear_left, rear_right, all_front, all_rear, left, right, all):
  - 空调 temperature: 13/7/4/4/4/4/4/4/5
  - 车窗: 11/4/4/3/3/4/4/4/5
  - 座椅: 14/5/3/3/4/4/5/3/5
  - Every bucket has at least 2 zone phrasings across the set.
- **Boundaries:** all present (temperature 16/32/22.5, fan level 1/7, volume 1/40, percent 50, seat level 1/3, brightness 0/100). Window percent 0 and 100 are labelled `close`/`open` under the window rule, so they never appear as `set`.

**Gates (final run):**
1. **Labels:** 0 failures.
2. **Duplicates:** 0 duplicate utterances; largest (tool, args) group is 3 rows.
3. **Near-duplicates:** 0 hits at ratio ≥ 0.7 against 4011 sentences (E4, D6 e1/e2/e3, train-v7a); max ratio 0.692. The first build had 176 flagged rows. I rewrote 174 by hand and dropped 2. I also dropped one unflagged row to stay at 400 or fewer.
4. **Coverage:** every tool × action cell has at least 3 rows except the 2 skips below. `coverage_matrix.py`'s per-action × bucket P-cell list is attached in `check_heldout.out` but was not used as a target.
5. **Prefix/suffix concentration:** worst 4/116 (polite) against the 12% limit.

**Uncovered (deliberately skipped):** 灯光/ambient `set_color_temp` and 灯光/reading `set_color`. The interface defines `set_color` for 氛围灯 only and `set_color_temp` for 阅读灯 only.

**Interface problems found (nothing changed):**
- 空调 `value` uses one range, 0.5 to 32, for both `set_temperature` and the fan counts. So the validator cannot reject a temperature below 16; this set has none.
- 座椅 increase/decrease carry the delta in `level` (range 1 to 3). "加两档" is labelled `level` 2.
- `coverage_matrix.py` still lists 空调 `increase`/`decrease` in TEMP_ACTIONS. They are not in the enum, so it has no effect.

**sha256:** `b1631035cd44cce6f71afdba0a5d6f5736860fd7a4b33dd4ea15f0e870e596fd  heldout-d10.jsonl` (also in `heldout-d10.sha256`).

I opened no forbidden file and committed nothing. No one has yet reviewed the wording or label semantics; the spec requires that review before the set is frozen.

Files are in /Users/youxun/project/edgeAgent-r217/docs/proposals/mcp-tool-redesign-r217/data/d10/heldout/:

<result>Fixed: row 64 is now the only row that changed. Its utterance is 请导航东港机场一号航站楼 and its label destination is 东港机场一号航站楼. `selftest.sh` exits 0 and passes every gate, including the new one.

**Cause.** The `ds` list in `build_d10e.py` was correct. The frame in `vocab_d10e.json` "nav.plain" (请导航东港机场航站楼) was missing 一号. I changed the frame and left the builder alone. I did not touch `build_d10e.py`.

**Diff.** `diff d10e-v6-before-dest-fix.jsonl d10e-new.jsonl` shows exactly one changed line (`64c64`). Only the `utterance` field differs, from 请导航东港机场航站楼 to 请导航东港机场一号航站楼. The `new_args`, `new_assistant_text`, `template_id` (`na_plain_3`) and `family` (`polite_question`) are identical. `template_ids.tsv` is unchanged, and the file still has 192 rows.

**New gate.** `G15_label_text_spoken_in_utterance` is added to the embedded Python block in `selftest.sh`. It asserts that every `destination` or `query` value in `new_args` is a substring of its row's `utterance`, for every row.
- New file: 0 violations, and the gate output is `G15_label_text_spoken_in_utterance=0_violations`.
- Old file (negative check): the same logic finds exactly one violation, `('东港机场一号航站楼', '请导航东港机场航站楼')`. The new gate would have caught this bug.
- Existing gate G11 only checked `search_play` queries, so it missed this. G15 covers `destination` too.

**Normalised queries.** There are none. The builder strips only the leading verb (放点, 播放, 来首, 搜一下, 来点 or 搜流行) from the 12 `search_play` utterances. The query is always a substring of the utterance, so nothing is exempted from G15. The file has 12 `search_play` rows and 54 `go` rows.

**Selftest.** The final lines of `gates.txt`:
```
built_rows=192
G1_validate_and_emit=0_errors
G2_group_template_caps=pass
G3_enum_position_checks=pass
G4_near_duplicates=0
G5_distinct_utterances=192/192

G8_median=8.0 p90=10 max=14
G10_family_recompute=0_mismatches
G12_numbered_level_conflicts=0
G11_addendum4_noun_query_checks=0_errors
G14_first_word_max_share=pass
G15_label_text_spoken_in_utterance=0_violations
near_duplicate_gate_correct_paths=0
```
- Near-duplicates against the training set, E4, D6 and held-out are 0 on both near-duplicate gates.
- The closer-share (G7) and first-word (G14) gates pass.
- The coverage matrix step also ran with exit 0.
- Before the edit I rebuilt from the old vocab to a temp file and `cmp` showed it identical to `d10e-new.jsonl`. This confirms the builder is deterministic, so the one-line diff comes only from the vocab edit.

**Files, all under `/Users/youxun/project/edgeAgent-r217/docs/proposals/mcp-tool-redesign-r217/`.** I ran no `git add`, `commit` or `stash`.
- `data/d10/e_mediavol/vocab_d10e.json` (edited)
- `data/d10/e_mediavol/selftest.sh` (G15 added)

**Verification**
1. **Run on current files.** `f` was rewritten at 04:23:18, during my first runs. The numbers below come from the final run on the new `f` (100 rows). Two consecutive runs on that fixed input gave byte-identical `kept.jsonl` and `FILTER-d10.json`. kept.jsonl sha256 is `e8fd27c349f5e21af2c92f583fe6621d5c453d10b8e677f3b004d2ae81e23a99`.
2. **Negative test.** I called `filter_rows` on a temporary inputs directory under /tmp, since removed:
   - The valid row was kept.
   - The row with position "driver" was dropped as `driver_position`.
   - The row copied from E4 was dropped as `leak_e4` (ratio 1.0, `data/d9/e4-frozen.jsonl`).
   - The row copied from train-v7a was dropped as `train_dup` (exact copy).
   - The same utterance with a different label raised `LabelConflict`, and `main()` turns that into exit 3 after printing both rows.
   - Extra checks: an exact duplicate with the same label is dropped as `dup_utterance`. Five rows with the same free-value group keep 3 and drop 2 as `group_cap`. Five rows whose args have no free value are all kept.
3. **Per-source table**

| source | in | kept | dropped by reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| a | 179 | 179 | none |
| b | 152 | 152 | none |
| c | 440 | 356 | leak_e2 42, leak_heldout 18, group_cap 17, train_dup 4, leak_e4 2, leak_e1 1 |
| d | 179 | 179 | none |
| e | 192 | 190 | group_cap 2 |
| f | 100 | 91 | leak_heldout 7, leak_e2 2 |
| total | 1242 | 1147 | leak_e2 44, leak_heldout 25, group_cap 19, train_dup 4, leak_e4 2, leak_e1 1 |

No row failed label validity, position "driver", or duplicate checks.

**coverage.txt** (train-v7a plus kept, `--min 12 --min-pos 4`: 4324 rows, 3484 calls, 840 no-call)
- **Cells under 12 rows, 6 of 86:**
  - 座椅/heating increase: 6
  - 座椅/heating decrease: 6
  - 座椅/ventilation increase: 6
  - 座椅/ventilation decrease: 6

  - 灯光/reading set_color: 0

**Judgment calls**
- **Label checks.** The no-call check is stricter than you specified: the text must also contain no `&lt;tool_call&gt;` and not be a bare `&lt;|im_end|&gt;`.
- **Label-conflict scope.** The conflict check runs on every row that passes rule 1, before the leak and train rules. A conflicting pair therefore stays fatal even if one of the two would later be dropped as a leak.
- **Stats for absent f.** If `f` is absent, `FILTER-d10.json` records it as absent with zero counts and the other sources still run.
- **Group-cap reference.** For `group_cap`, the `reference` field in `dropped.jsonl` lists the `source:line` of the 3 rows already kept in that group. It has no utterance or ratio.</result>

<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-youxun-project-edgeAgent/a0933d46-6470-4d0e-8044-d6b32f7a2916/tasks/b10q4pwvc.output</output-file>
<status>completed</status>
<summary>Background command "export PATH="/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:$PATH"
T=$HOME/.local/share/tts-training; B=/Users/youxun/project/edgeAgent-artifacts/tts-device-bench-20261109; mkdir -p $B/style
cat &gt; $B/style_bench.py &lt;&lt;'EOF'
# Sampling A/B on MPS for Qwen3-TTS 0.6B CustomVoice. Same texts, same seed. Measures prosodic "drama".
import sys,time,json,os,numpy as np,soundfile as sf,torch
from qwen_tts import Qwen3TTSModel
out=sys.argv[1]; root=os.path.expanduser("~/.local/share/tts-training/models/Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-0.6B-CustomVoice")
TEXTS={"S1":"当前车速一百二十公里，请注意限速。","S2":"已经为您规划路线，预计二十分钟到达。","S3":"空调温度已设为二十六度。","S4":"前方五百米右转，然后保持直行。"}
CFG={
 "default":dict(),
 "t0.5":dict(temperature=0.5,subtalker_temperature=0.5),
 "t0.3":dict(temperature=0.3,subtalker_temperature=0.3,top_k=20,subtalker_top_k=20),
 "greedy":dict(do_sample=False,subtalker_dosample=False),
}
m=Qwen3TTSModel.from_pretrained(root,device_map="mps",dtype=torch.float32)
rows=[]
for spk in("uncle_fu","vivian"):
  for cn,kw in CFG.items():
    if spk=="vivian" and cn not in("default","greedy"): continue
    for k,t in TEXTS.items():
        torch.manual_seed(1); torch.mps.synchronize(); t0=time.time()
        try: w,sr=m.generate_custom_voice(text=t,language="Chinese",speaker=spk,non_streaming_mode=True,instruct="",**kw)
        except Exception as e: print("FAIL",spk,cn,k,repr(e)[:150]); continue
        torch.mps.synchronize(); x=np.asarray(w[0],dtype=np.float32)
        p=f"{out}/style/{spk}_{cn}_{k}.wav"; sf.write(p,x,sr)
        rows.append(dict(spk=spk,cfg=cn,id=k,wall=round(time.time()-t0,2),dur=round(len(x)/sr,2),path=p)); print(rows[-1],flush=True)
json.dump(rows,open(f"{out}/style.json","w"),ensure_ascii=False,indent=1)
EOF
cd $B &amp;&amp; $T/venv-qwen/bin/python style_bench.py $B 2&gt;&amp;1 | rg "^\{|FAIL|Error" | cut -c1-170" completed (exit code 0)</summary>
</task-notification>
